\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 \strokec2 4. Branch Scope and Production Context\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 The Dance branch covers Dance audition assessment inside TapeCoach\'92s existing shared six-field architecture. It improves Dance-specific report behaviour by adding style-aware movement language, evidence anchors, assessability preconditions, live-room-only claim boundaries, anti-bias protections and user-facing label containment for the overloaded 
\f2\fs26 vocal
\f1\fs24  field.\
The revised Dance branch is intended to improve:\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls1\ilvl0\cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 credibility of Dance reports;\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 movement-technique specificity;\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 full-body/task-sufficient visibility handling;\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 music/audio assessability handling;\

\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 generic-feedback suppression;\

\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 accessibility-safe and adapted-movement language;\

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 \strokec2 15. Compact Branch Handoff Pack\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 The Dance branch is complete and ready for cross-branch consolidation. It has completed source census, four extraction batches, synthesis, gap audit, revision, final audit, output-specificity mapping, v5 maturity review and pre-production QA planning. The branch uses stable IDs from DANCE-S001\'96DANCE-S072, DANCE-SYN-F01\'96F14, DANCE-AUDIT-I01\'96I14, DANCE-AUDIT-R01\'96R14, DANCE-REV-C01\'96C14, DANCE-REV-S01\'96S12 and DANCE-OS-R01\'96R14 / DANCE-OS-T01\'96T15.\
The strongest source basis comes from admissions/school sources for process and video requirements, RAD/ISTD formal sources for descriptor language, accessibility/self-tape sources for non-penalisation and assessability boundaries, and employer/company sources for professional context. The key synthesis conclusion is that Dance cannot be treated as one generic movement standard. Dance outputs must be style-specific where justified, cautious where style is mixed or unclear, and grounded in observable movement evidence.\
The accepted revision improves Dance at wording and guardrail level without changing the shared architecture. It contains the 
\f2\fs26 vocal
\f1\fs24  proxy risk by requiring Dance-only user-facing labels to mean movement/dance technique, not singing or voice. It codifies assessability preconditions, blocks generic praise, ring-fences live-room-only claims, strengthens accessibility-safe assessment, constrains professional_presentation to assessability/brief response/safe preparation, and adds timestamp/reliability expectations.\
The final audit approved Dance with explicit limitations. The branch is not yet production-verified because no Dance-only live reports, PDFs, JSONs or comparison examples exist. The uploaded outputs are Musical Theatre examples and cannot verify Dance behaviour. The pre-production QA plan therefore requires synthetic Dance outputs before release, with P0 tests for label containment, home setup, polish bias, cropped visibility, weak audio, tap audibility, adapted movement, pickup/stamina overclaim and unknown-style fallback.\
No further Dance research or rubric rewrite is required before cross-branch consolidation. Product release should wait for pre-production synthetic QA and later first-output monitoring.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0
